22 11:03:52       Clause 14.16, the set-off clause, we say is a full
23 11:03:59   answer to Mr Ortega's point.  It says:
24 11:04:02       "The employer may, to the extent permitted by law,
25 11:04:06   retain or set off any amounts which are or may be
                                      77
 1 11:04:10   payable by the contractor to the employer against
 2 11:04:13   amounts that may become or are payable to the contractor
 3 11:04:16   (including security) under the contract."
 4 11:04:19       Members of the tribunal, experienced as you are in
 5 11:04:28   construction contracts, you will appreciate that
 6 11:04:30   a retention under a construction contract relies upon
 7 11:04:37   the existence of a set-off clause such as this.  Because
 8 11:04:43   the very point of having a retention is that in the
 9 11:04:48   event the contractor is late, is liable for liquidated
10 11:04:53   damages, in the event that there are defects which
11 11:04:59   entitle the employer to damages, the retention is there
12 11:05:02   to ensure that there is a sum that can be set off
13 11:05:08   against the contractor's right to payment.
14 11:05:11       That is the point of having a retention.
15 11:05:16       The retention relies upon a contractual mechanism
16 11:05:19   such as 14.16, to work.  Otherwise, there would be no
17 11:05:24   retention.
18 11:05:27       What I think Mr Ortega is suggesting to you is that
19 11:05:32   the retention should automatically be paid out,
20 11:05:36   regardless of the claims that the employer has against
21 11:05:41   the contractor.  But that's just not how this contract
22 11:05:44   works.  Indeed, that's not how any contract, including
23 11:05:49   a retention provision, could sensibly work.
24 11:05:56       This is not a clever invention of leading counsel.
25 11:06:05   This is in fact something that Legoland itself said.  If

24 11:37:53   together, please.
25 11:37:54       "Welding quality of PPR pipe is an important
                                      93
 1 11:37:58   criterion for its satisfactory service condition.
 2 11:38:01   Various factors that affect the welding quality includes
 3 11:38:04   welding temperature and time, welding depth, quality of
 4 11:38:07   raw material etc.  While fabricating the PPR fitting,
 5 11:38:11   welding temperature has to be controlled at 260-270
 6 11:38:15   degrees Celsius.  When the temperature is too low, the
 7 11:38:19   PPR pipe cannot be melted in place, and the pipe and
 8 11:38:22   fittings cannot be welded well.  When the temperature is
 9 11:38:25   too high, the PPR pipe will melt too much, so that it is
10 11:38:28   easy to cause blockage at the joint of the pipe or the
11 11:38:31   pipe hole is too small to affect the flow.  Moreover it
12 11:38:35   will affect the welding quality which can cause leakage
13 11:38:37   and decrease service life of the sample.  From the below
14 11:38:40   images, uneven welding sites can be observed.  This
15 11:38:44   could be a reason for leakages in pipes."
16 11:38:49       Then this is probably the most important bit:
17 11:38:51       "From DSC analysis it is been found that material
18 11:38:54   melting point is less than standard PPR pipes.  Lower
19 11:38:58   the initial melting point, the faster the material will
20 11:39:01   melt, and the higher the melting point, the thinner the
21 11:39:04   material will melt.  These two characteristics directly
22 11:39:08   affect the operation time of PPR pipe welding and
23 11:39:12   further affect the service life of the material."

20 15:34:18       I don't have a copy of the Civil Code in front of

22 15:34:25   The way that Article 392 works is that a party needs to
23 15:34:31   make an application to the tribunal or a court to
24 15:34:36   replace the agreed level of liquidated damages with the
25 15:34:41   level of actual loss.
                                     184
 1 15:34:45       So it is the claimant's application to make, to

 3 15:34:57   members of the tribunal, have a discretion as to whether

 5 15:35:02       In order to make the application, as is the case for
 6 15:35:10   any applicant, the claimant will need to prove, the
 7 15:35:17   burden is on the claimant to show, that we have suffered
 8 15:35:21   no loss.

14 15:35:48   I submit is a very basic proposition, that if a hotel
15 15:35:52   doesn't open in, say, January, but opens in November,
16 15:35:59   then it will have lost out on 11 months of the profit
17 15:36:03   that it would have earned from its normal operation.

22 15:36:27   tower block which is going to be sold unit by unit.  But

                                     185
 1 15:36:46   because the construction works are delayed, then there
